&\qquad -\frac{i}{32}F{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\beta}{}_{\eta}\varepsilon{}^{r j}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{c}W{}^{\delta}{}_{\gamma}\big)\lambda{}_{\delta}{}^{i} -\frac{i}{32}F{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\beta}{}_{\eta}\varepsilon{}^{r j}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}^{\gamma \delta}\big(\nabla_{c}W{}_{\beta \delta}\big)\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{i}\\
